nomodule=""></script><script data-cookieconsent="ignore" id="plausible-init">
    // A saved login is the auth cookie itself or one of its numbered chunks
    // (name + ".0"). PKCE helper cookies (name + "-code-verifier") do not
    // count, so a guest who only requested a magic link stays a guest.
    (function() {
      var authCookieName = "sb-olfbykzrdbbfinnalniv-auth-token";
      window.__remotelyHasSavedLogin = function() {
        try {
          if (typeof document === 'undefined' || !document) return false;
          return String(document.cookie || '').split(';').some(function(entry) {
            var separator = entry.indexOf('=');
            var name = (separator === -1 ? entry : entry.slice(0, separator)).trim();
            if (name === authCookieName) return true;
            return (
              name.indexOf(authCookieName + '.') === 0 &&
              /^[0-9]+$/.test(name.slice(authCookieName.length + 1))
            );
          });
        } catch (e) {
          return false;
        }
      };
    })();
    window.plausible = window.plausible || function() {
      (window.plausible.q = window.plausible.q || []).push(arguments);
    };
    window.plausible.init = window.plausible.init || function(options) {
      window.plausible.o = options || {};
    };
    window.plausible.init({
      // Auto-captured pageviews never pass through the analytics dispatcher,
      // so attach consent_state here. Keep this aligned with
      // resolveConsentState() in src/lib/consent/cookiebot.ts.
      // user_logged_in and page_frame go on every event; event props win.
      // page_frame names the frame this HTML build renders on listing, job
      // and company pages. Other paths do not use this value for frame reports.
      customProperties: function(eventName) {
        var sharedProperties = {
          user_logged_in: window.__remotelyHasSavedLogin(),
          page_frame: 'app'
        };
        // The account frame sets one exp_ property per running test.
        // Only short lower-case names and values pass.
        var testProperties = [];
        try {
          var groups = window.__remotelyTestProperties;
          if (groups && typeof groups === 'object') {
            Object.keys(groups).forEach(function(name) {
              var value = groups[name];
              if (/^exp_[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$/.test(name) && typeof value === 'string' && /^[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$/.test(value)) {
                sharedProperties[name] = value;
                testProperties.push(name);
              }
            });
          }
        } catch (e) {}
        if (eventName !== 'pageview') return sharedProperties;
        var consentState = 'unknown';
        try {
          var cb = window.Cookiebot;
          if (cb) {
            var hasResponse = cb.hasResponse === true;
            var declined = cb.declined === true;
            var consent = cb.consent;
            var ready = hasResponse || declined || (consent && typeof consent === 'object');
            if (ready) {
              if (consent && consent.statistics === true) {
                consentState = 'granted';
              } else if (declined || hasResponse) {
                consentState = 'denied';
              }
            }
          }
        } catch (e) {}
        var pageviewProperties = {
          consent_state: consentState,
          user_logged_in: sharedProperties.user_logged_in,
          page_frame: sharedProperties.page_frame
        };
        testProperties.forEach(function(name) { pageviewProperties[name] = sharedProperties[name]; });
        // The homepage hero cohort travels on the homepage pageview. The path
        // is the source of truth, so a client navigation to "/" is also
        // covered. A DOM read would depend on render timing.
        try {
          if (window.location && window.location.pathname === '/') {
            var entrySurface = {"entry_surface":"homepage","entry_surface_version":"search_hero_v2","entry_release_id":"2026-09-04_homepage_search_hero_v2"};
            pageviewProperties.entry_surface = entrySurface.entry_surface;
            pageviewProperties.entry_surface_version = entrySurface.entry_surface_version;
            pageviewProperties.entry_release_id = entrySurface.entry_release_id;
          }
        } catch (e) {}
        return pageviewProperties;
      },
      // Plausible copies a tagged anchor href into p.url. Rebuild application
      // properties from the closed contract so no arbitrary employer or DOM
      // values can be transmitted. Incomplete events are dropped; retaining the
      // event-name class on their anchors prevents automatic outbound fallback.
      transformRequest: function(payload) {
        if (!payload) return payload;
        var withoutPrivateSetupUrl = function a(b){try{let c=new URL(b,"https://privacy.invalid"),d=!1;for(let a of["q","location","lat","lon"])c.searchParams.has(a)&&(c.searchParams.delete(a),d=!0);for(let[b,e]of c.searchParams)if(/^(?:https?:\/\/|\/)/i.test(e)){let f=a(e);f!==e&&(c.searchParams.set(b,f),d=!0)}if(!d)return b;return/^https?:\/\//i.test(b)?c.href:`${c.pathname}${c.search}${c.hash}`}catch{return b}};
        if (typeof payload.u === 'string') payload.u = withoutPrivateSetupUrl(payload.u);
        if (typeof payload.r === 'string') payload.r = withoutPrivateSetupUrl(payload.r);
        if (payload.n !== 'job_apply_clicked') return payload;

        try {
          var properties = payload.p;
          if (!properties || typeof properties !== 'object') return null;

          var jobId = properties.job_id;
          var surface = properties.surface;
          var numericJobId = typeof jobId === 'string' ? Number(jobId) : NaN;
          var validJobId =
            typeof jobId === 'string' &&
            /^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(jobId) &&
            Number.isSafeInteger(numericJobId);
          var validSurface =
            surface === 'job_detail' ||
            surface === 'saved_jobs' ||
            surface === 'cv_match_detail';
          if (!validJobId || !validSurface) return null;

          var sanitizedProperties = {
            job_id: jobId,
            surface: surface
          };
          // Kept for a guest-only apply rate; unknown values are dropped.
          if (typeof properties.user_logged_in === 'boolean') {
            sanitizedProperties.user_logged_in = properties.user_logged_in;
          }
          if (properties.page_frame === 'marketing' || properties.page_frame === 'app') {
            sanitizedProperties.page_frame = properties.page_frame;
          }
          // The match area seen on the list card (closed values; a band only for numeric scores).
          var matchState = properties.match_state;
          if (matchState === 'picked' || matchState === 'value' || matchState === 'locked' || matchState === 'needs_role' || matchState === 'none') {
            sanitizedProperties.match_state = matchState;
            var scoreBand = properties.score_band;
            if (matchState === 'value' && (scoreBand === '0_64' || scoreBand === '65_79' || scoreBand === '80_100')) {
              sanitizedProperties.score_band = scoreBand;
            }
          }
          payload.p = sanitizedProperties;
          if (payload.p !== sanitizedProperties) return null;
        } catch (e) {
          return null;
        }

        return payload;
      }
    });

    // The Plausible tracker patches history.pushState only. Next.js enters a
    // route with history.replaceState for router.replace() and for server
    // redirects, so those arrivals never reach analytics. Report them here.
    // The tracker keeps ownership of pushState, so no view is counted twice.
    (function() {
      try {
        var browserHistory = window.history;
        if (!browserHistory || typeof browserHistory.replaceState !== 'function') return;
        if (browserHistory.plausibleReplaceStatePatched === true) return;

        var originalReplaceState = browserHistory.replaceState;
        browserHistory.replaceState = function() {
          var previousPath = window.location ? window.location.pathname : null;
          // An address correction (job id to job slug) is the same page view; consume the one-shot mark.
          var silent = window.__remotelySilentAddressChange === true;
          window.__remotelySilentAddressChange = false;
          var result = originalReplaceState.apply(this, arguments);
          try {
            var nextPath = window.location ? window.location.pathname : null;
            // Only a path change is a new page. Query-string rewrites and
            // scroll restoration keep the same path and stay silent.
            if (!silent && previousPath && nextPath && previousPath !== nextPath) {
              window.plausible('pageview');
            }
          } catch (e) {}
          return result;
        };
        browserHistory.plausibleReplaceStatePatched = true;
      } catch (e) {}
    })();
  </script><style type="text/css">[data-vaul-drawer]{touch-action:none;will-change:transform;transition:transform .5s cubic-bezier(.32, .72, 0, trabalhando diretamente com times de Cloud, Engenharia, Arquitetura, Cyber e Operação.\n\nÉ uma posição hands-on, com espaço para construir, automatizar e definir padrões e não apenas administrar uma ferramenta.\n\n* * *\n\n## Principais responsabilidades\n\n*   Evoluir a arquitetura e os controles de Cloud Security em ambientes multicloud\n*   Estruturar e evoluir capacidades de CSPM, CIEM, Cloud Workload Security e Cloud Exposure Management.\n*   Atuar tecnicamente incluindo configuração, políticas, regras, integrações, priorização e tratamento de riscos.\n*   Construir security guardrails e padrões de Cloud Security.\n*   Integrar controles de segurança ao SDLC, CI/CD e DevSecOps.\n*   Desenvolver automações utilizando Skills, APIs e/ou outras tecnologias.\n*   Trabalhar com Infrastructure as Code, especialmente Terraform, incorporando segurança desde a construção da infraestrutura.\n*   Atuar na segurança de containers e Kubernetes.\n*   Integrar Cloud Security com IAM, DevOps, ITSM, observabilidade e demais capacidades de Cyber.\n*   Desenvolver mecanismos de priorização de riscos considerando exposição, criticidade, contexto e impacto, reduzindo falsos positivos e esforço operacional.\n*   Apoiar a definição de Secure Cloud Patterns e arquiteturas de referência.\n*   Transformar controles de segurança em capacidades reutilizáveis para os times de Engenharia.\n*   Apoiar em métricas e mecanismos de acompanhamento da evolução da postura de segurança Cloud.\n*   Atuar como referência técnica em Cloud Security junto às equipes de Engenharia e Arquitetura.\n\n* * Groups/NSGs, NACLs e firewalls;\n*   Segmentação e controle de tráfego;\n*   Ingress/egress e exposição à Internet;\n*   NAT, proxies, DNS e Load Balancers;\n*   WAF e controles de exposição;\n*   VPN, VPC/VNet, subnets, routing e conectividade híbrida;\n*   Interconnect/ExpressRoute/Direct Connect;\n*   Princípios de Zero Trust e microsegmentação;\n*   Cloud IAM e princípios de least               return;
            }

          function scheduleDeferredLoad(callback) {
            var loaded = false;

            function trigger() {
              if (loaded) return;
              loaded = true;
              cleanup();
              runWhenIdle(callback);
            }

            function cleanup() {
              w.removeEventListener('scroll', trigger, passiveOnce);
              w.removeEventListener('click', trigger);
              w.removeEventListener('keydown', trigger);
              w.removeEventListener('touchstart', trigger, passiveOnce);

              w.setTimeout(trigger, DEFER_MS);
